HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B013"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1fafc2c65d2b096c9ce4","text":"3. **Extension converted into trapped pressure.** Fixed `عَمَدٍ` activates `ع م د B013`, blocking a stream so its flow gathers in one place, while fixed `مُّمَدَّدَةٍ` activates `م د د B003`, water flowing, swelling, and being fed by more water. Sealing in `104:8`, `و ص د B001`, makes the branch-pair spatially coherent. The packet supplies extension, blockage, and closure; the hydraulic transfer to heat and bodies is my abductive move. The phrase can therefore read as a dam-like mechanism in which continuing movement cannot disperse and instead accumulates as internal pressure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001407/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2b213a4b19610ad93eed","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:9:3\",\"root_001407\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Aranan ölümsüzlük, yaşamın uzaması olarak değil, cezanın süresinin uzaması olarak geri döner.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B013","root_001407/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3c7d0952ab872658b5c0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:9:2\",\"root_001043\",[\"B013\"]],[\"104:9:3\",\"root_001407\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Sütunlar, engellenmiş bir akışın dağılmayıp içeride basınç biriktirmesi gibi, uzamayı hapsedilmiş kuvvete dönüştüren bir düzenek olarak okunabilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_41b440215071dfc7ac4c","text":"1. **An extended fastening architecture.** Fixed `عَمَدٍ` directly realizes `ع م د B003`, concrete pillars, poles, or columns, and fixed `مُّمَدَّدَةٍ` realizes `م د د B001`, lengthwise drawing-out. Prior `مُّؤْصَدَةٌ`, `و ص د B001`, supplies a sealed door-state. The packet gives closure followed by elongated structural members; I infer that the members brace or bar the closure rather than merely decorate its surroundings. The final phrase thus reads as the mechanical completion of confinement: the seal is given stable length, leverage, and resistance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_55d38a2115905d6a9719","text":"No later ayah exists in the packet, so no later-ayah surprise emerged. The retrospective comparison with the preceding sections nevertheless confirmed that the retained readings remain anchored to fixed `ع م د` and `م د د`: columns, support, blocked flow, and directed extension each explain a different function of the final structure without requiring a new external theme.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6163b8f4fd8ea82d2f1a","text":"1. **Extended architecture of confinement.** Evidence: `ع م د B003` (pillar/column) and `م د د B001` (lengthwise extension). Structural cue: this final phrase follows مؤصدة and specifies the sealed state spatially. Assigned role: عمد are the supports; ممددة stretches them into a sustained structure. Abductive move: closure requires an architecture that holds pressure in place. Reading change: the final ayah is not decorative scenery; it gives the mechanics of enclosure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_675e32c21c6102ecc717","text":"5. **Somatic pressure as an exploratory undertone.** Evidence: `ع م د B008` (crushing affliction) and `ع م د B009` (pressed wound/swelling), activated by `ف ء د B003` and `ح ط م B001`. Structural cue: the final columns come after heart-striking fire and crushing. Assigned role: عمد adds bodily strain beneath the architecture. Abductive move: spatial confinement and bodily affliction can be read as two images of the same pressure. Reading change: the ending can be felt not only as a building around them but as a sustained pressure through them; this is exploratory because the surface noun strongly favors the column/support branch.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000945/B006","root_001043/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_80c3cd6d16efea28b739","text":"4. **Exploratory directed lines toward the inward target.** Fixed `عَمَدٍ` activates `ع م د B001`, deliberate aiming, while fixed `مُّمَدَّدَةٍ` supplies elongated lines through `م د د B001`. `104:7` supplies hearts as the endpoint and `ط ل ع B006` as the route toward them. The columns are not grammatically said to point at the hearts, so that directional arrow is inferred. If retained, the final image is not only enclosure around persons but a deliberately aligned structure whose extensions preserve the targeting established two ayat earlier.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B001","root_001407/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8b0d1173cc1c12f3ea04","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:9:2\",\"root_001043\",[\"B001\"]],[\"104:9:3\",\"root_001407\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Uzatılmış doğruların yüreklere doğru amaçlı biçimde yöneldiği ve önceki hedeflemeyi yapısal olarak sürdürdüğü ileri sürülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8bff2414ed6c07fdfdd3","text":"3. **False support reversed into prison support.** Evidence: `ع م د B002` (propping/support), `ع م د B006` (mainstay relied upon), and `ح س ب B003` (sufficiency), activated by `م و ل B001` in 104:2-3. Structural cue: the man relied on wealth; the ending shows supports that hold him. Assigned role: عماد becomes the replacement for the support he trusted. Abductive move: what one relies on wrongly returns as the structure that immobilizes rather than upholds. Reading change: \"pillars\" are read as reversed reliance: support becomes captivity.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B002","root_001043/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9b1ce11fd16d19ca6797","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:9:2\",\"root_001043\",[\"B002\",\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Servetin güvenilir dayanak sayılması tersine çevrilir: bu kez destek, kişiyi ayakta tutmak yerine içeride tutan yapıya aittir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001407/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9e95ac8a1f6a5d9d692a","text":"4. **Extended time answering the immortality fantasy.** Evidence: `م د د B004` (extended time/term/respite), activated by `خ ل د B001` in 104:3 and `ع د د B005` (counted time/recurrence) in 104:2. Structural cue: the last word is an extended participle after claims of permanence and counting. Assigned role: ممددة supplies duration as well as length. Abductive move: the text lets the desire for extension survive only as extended punishment. Reading change: the final phrase answers \"أخلده\" by turning temporal extension into dread rather than security.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B002","root_001407/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a88e633152544faa0865","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:9:2\",\"root_001043\",[\"B002\"]],[\"104:9:3\",\"root_001407\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Destek ve uzatma, kapatma ve yakma düzeninin tükenmeden sürmesini sağlayan devamlı bir besleme altyapısı ima eder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b2d7ded17196e6c86a53","text":"No later ayah remains. The retrospective surprise is instead convergent: 104:9 gathers earlier threads—counted units, false permanence, sealed closure, rising fire, and bodily pressure—into one final image of extended, intentional support that imprisons rather than saves.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cc5dc7f7ed951d518ff9","text":"2. **Deliberate targeting made structural.** Evidence: `ع م د B001` (intentional aiming), activated by `د ر ي B002` in 104:5 and `ط ل ع B003/B004` in 104:7. Structural cue: question, disclosure, and heart-targeting precede the pillars. Assigned role: عمد supplies intention inside the architecture. Abductive move: the final structure embodies the directionality already supplied by knowledge and rising disclosure. Reading change: the punishment is not merely spatial; it is aimed and purposive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_da7ae02a941ba72b4118","text":"2. **A punishment continuously supported and supplied.** Fixed `عَمَدٍ` activates `ع م د B002`, propping something with a support, while fixed `مُّمَدَّدَةٍ` activates `م د د B002`, connected increase that reinforces or supplies another thing. The nearby kindled fire supplies `و ق د B001` and its fuel-requiring branch `B002`. The packet does not state what the columns supply; I infer that their extended network maintains the enclosing and kindling system. On this reading, the phrase describes infrastructural continuity: the apparatus is propped and resupplied rather than allowed to exhaust itself.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B008","root_001043/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-7","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_db9ddd868cef2c4e2e30","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:9:2\",\"root_001043\",[\"B008\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Sütunların, çevredeki mimari olmaktan çıkıp bedene işleyen ezici ve sürekli bir baskıyı da taşıdığı ileri sürülür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001043/B003","root_001407/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f09ce6f862a9c1e91958","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:9:2\",\"root_001043\",[\"B003\"]],[\"104:9:3\",\"root_001407\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Uzatılmış sütunlar, önceki kapanmayı destekleyip berkiterek hapsin mekanik tamamlanışını sağlar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
